./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:## LOW-LEVEL ROUTINE -- NOT NORMALLY FOR END USERS -- USE WITH CAUTION
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:## NOTE: PG_answer_eval	is used	instead	of PG_restricted_eval in order to insure that the answer
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:## evaluated within	the	context	of the package the problem was originally defined in.
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:## Includes multivariable modifications contributed by Professor William K. Ziemer
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:## IN:	a hash consisting of the following keys (error checking to be added later?)
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			correctEqn			--	the correct equation as a string
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			var				--	the variable name as a string,
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##								or a reference to an array of variables
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			limits				--	reference to an array of arrays of type [lower,upper]
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			tolerance			--	the allowable margin of error
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			tolType				--	'relative' or 'absolute'
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			numPoints			--	the number of points to evaluate the function at
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			mode				--	'std' or 'antider'
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			maxConstantOfIntegration	--	maximum size of the constant of integration
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			zeroLevel			--	if the correct answer is this close to zero,
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##												then zeroLevelTol applies
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			zeroLevelTol			--	absolute tolerance to allow when answer is close to zero
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##			test_points			--	user supplied points to use for testing the
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##                          function, either array of arrays, or optionally
./answers/PGfunctionevaluators.pl:##                          reference to single array (for one variable)

./answers/weightedGrader.pl:##################################################
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  Issue ANS() calls for the weighted checkers
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:##################################################
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  Issue NAMED_ANS() calls for the weighted checkers
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:##################################################
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  Issue an ANS() call for the checker, giving
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  credit to the given answers.
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:##################################################
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  Either add a weight to an AnswerEvaluator, or return a
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  new checker that adds the weight to its result.  Also,
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  add the "credit" field, if supplied.
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:##################################################
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  This is the weighted grader.  It uses an extra field added to the
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  AnswerHash (named "weight") to tell how much weight to give each
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  problem.  The grader adds up the total weights for the correct
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  answers.  For partially correct ones, it uses the score for that
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  answer to give a portion of that weight.  For example, if the
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  weight is 40 and the score for the answer is .5, then 20 is added
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  to the total for the problem.  (Note that most answer checkers only
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  return 1 or 0, but they are allowed to return partial credit as
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  well.)
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  When the student's total is computed, it is divided by the sum of
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  all the weights in order to determine the final score.
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  It also uses a special field called "credit" that determines
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  what other (named) answers are given full credit if the given
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  answer is correct.  This can be used to make "optional" answers,
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  where getting the "final" answer right gives credit for the other parts.
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:##################################################
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#  Syntactic sugar to avoid ugly ~~& construct in PG.
./answers/weightedGrader.pl:#

./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#  Low-level routine for handling unordered collections of answer checkers
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:##################################################
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#  AnswerChecker that allows a blank answer in a collection of unordered
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#  answer checkers.  It will return "correct" for a blank answer only if
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#  all the other answers are correct.  (The blankOK value is set by
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#  unordered_answer_list when this is true.)  This lets you ask a question
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#  where the number of answers is not known (by the student) ahead of time.
./answers/unorderedAnswer.pl:#

./PG.pl:# undefined means old style return (a simple string) failed
./PG.pl:# $obj->{success} defined but equal to zero means that the failed return and error
./PG.pl:# messages are encoded in the $obj hash.
./PG.pl:# The store_persistent_data, update_persistent_data, and get_persistent_data methods are deprecated and are only still
./PG.pl:# here for backward compatability. Use the persistent_data method instead which can do everything these three methods
./PG.pl:# can do. Note that if you use the persistent_data method, then you will need to join the values as strings if you want
./PG.pl:# that. Even better pass the persistent_data method an array reference containing the values so you can avoid the hassle
./PG.pl:# of splitting the values when they are retrieved.

./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#  Genarate a new AlignedList object
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#     $al = new_aligned_list(options)
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#  Where "options" can be taken from among:
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#      valign => "placement"     Sets the vertical alignment for the table
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#                                (default is valign => "MIDDLE")
./ui/alignedChoice.pl:#

./ui/text2PG.pl:######################################################################
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#  Sanitize a text string for use with TEXT and EV3, so that
./ui/text2PG.pl:#  non-math text is properly displayed in HTML mode and TeX
./ui/text2PG.pl:#  mode.  Newlines and blank lines can be converted to $BR and
./ui/text2PG.pl:#  $PAR automatically.
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#  Format:  text2PG(string [,options]);
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#  where options are from among:
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#    trimWhitespace => 0 or 1    Specifies if leading and trailing whitespace
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                is removed before processing.
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                  Default: 1
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#    convertBlanklines => 0 or 1 Specifies whether blank lines should be
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                turned into paragraph breaks.
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                  Default: 1
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#    convertNewlines => 0 or 1   Specifies whether newlines should be
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                turned into line breaks.
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                  Default: 1
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#    sanitizeText => 0 or 1      Specifies whether to convert HTML and TeX
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                special characters to printable form
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                outside of math mode and commands.
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                  Default: 1
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#    allowCommands => 0 or 1     Specifies if text within command blocks
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                is left unchanged (1) or not (0).
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                  Default: 0
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#    convertDollars => 0 or 1    Specifies whether dollar signs not followed
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                by a letter should be replaced by ${DOLLAR}
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                (you only want to do this if not passing
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                the result through EV3 with variable
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                substitutions).
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                  Default: 0
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./ui/text2PG.pl:#    doubleSlashes => 0 or 1     Specifies whether backslashes should be
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                doubled (in preparation for passing
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                into EV3).
./ui/text2PG.pl:#                                  Default: 1
./ui/text2PG.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# additional macros developed in conjunction with the problems from
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# the consortium (hughes-hallett) calculus text
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# written by Gavin LaRose, <glarose@umich.edu>
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# version 1.2
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# 1.2: added reduced_frac()
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# 1.1: added numorfun_cmp()
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# shufflemap
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# produces a reference to a hash of indices from 0 to n-1 pointing to
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# a random permutation of the same, and the index that points to 0
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# string_list_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# check a comma-separated list of strings as student answers and
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# check them with the standard str_cmp evaluator.  this is really
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# John Jones' number_list_cmp rewritten to be do string comparison
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# integrand_fun_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# an answer evaluator that allows checking functions with 'dt' in them;
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# redundant now that the Parser allows checking with multicharacter
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# variables
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# numfun_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# an answer evaluator that requires that the answer be
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# simplified to the number given without returning an error if
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# it's a function
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# numorfun_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# an answer evaluator that first checks to see if the student answer
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# checks correctly as a number, and if so returns that result; if not,
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# repeats the check with a function checker and returns that.  options
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# for the respective checkers are provided as separate hash references
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# classify_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# take a correct answer of the form "val=string, val=string.."
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# and check a comma-separated list of student answers against these
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# if the option 'order'=>'strict' is provided, requires that the
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# answers be in the same order.  any other options are passed to
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# num_cmp to check the val entries; strings are checked with a
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# standard str_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# doesn't currently allow for passing in of a reference to an array
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# of solutions to generate a list of evaluators, but that would be
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# easy to add
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# integrand_fun_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# an answer evaluator to allow checking functions with 'dt' in them
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# numfun_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# an answer evaluator that requires that the answer be
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# simplified to the number given without returning an error if
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# it's a function
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# numorfun_cmp
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# an answer evaluator that first checks to see if the student answer
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# checks correctly as a number, and if so returns that result; if not,
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:# repeats the check with a function checker and returns that.
./deprecated/hhAdditionalMacros.pl:#

./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#  Modify a polynomial to remove coeficients of 1, -1 and 0
./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#  The polynomial can be a multivariable one.  The parameters
./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#  following the formula itself are the names of the variables
./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#  for the formula.  Any number can be provided, and the default
./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#  is x, y, and z.  Variable names must be one character long,
./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#  and the formula really should be a polynomial, as the algorithm
./deprecated/unionMacros.pl:#  for removing coefficients of 0 relies on that in an important way.

./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# bkell_simplify_fraction($num, $denom)
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# Simplifies the fraction $num/$denom; returns the list ($num, $denom).
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:###############################################################################
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# bkell_simplify_fraction_string($num, $denom [, $flags])
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# Returns a string like "$num/$denom" in simplified form. If the string $flags
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# contains "+", then a leading "+" is included if the fraction is non-negative.
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# If $flags contains "0", then a fraction with a value of 0 will cause the
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# empty string to be returned. If $flags contains "1", then a fraction with a
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# value of 1 or -1 will cause only the sign to be returned (or the empty
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# string, if the fraction is non-negative and $flags does not contain "+"). If
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# $flags contains "(", then parentheses will be placed around "$num/$denom"
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# (the sign, if it exists, will be outside the parentheses); if $flags contains
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# "[", then parentheses will be used only if a sign is used and the denominator
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# is other than 1. A flag of "(" overrides "]". If $flags contains "f", then
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# the string returned will be in the form "{$num \over $denom}" instead of the
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# normal slashed version.
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:###############################################################################
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# bkell_poly_term($coeff_num, $coeff_denom, $var [, "+"])
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# Produces and simplifies a term of a polynomial of the general form
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# "($coeff_num/$coeff_denom)$var". Handles special cases (such as $num==0,
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# $denom==1, etc.). If the fourth argument is "+", then a leading "+" is
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# included if the term is positive.
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#

./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# bkell_graph_axis($a, $b)
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# Returns a list ($min, $max, $step), where $min <= $a, $max >= $b, $step is a
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# power of 10, $min and $max are multiples of $step, abs($min) <= 9*$step, and
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# abs($max) <= 9*$step. Useful for deciding bounds for the axis of a graph. For
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# example, to make a graph axis that can handle values between $a and $b, call
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# bkell_graph_axis, and then set the minimum value of the axis to $min and the
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:# maximum to $max, and put tick marks every $step units.
./deprecated/freemanMacros.pl:#

./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  This package implements a method of handling multi-part problems
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  that show only a single part at any one time.  The students can
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  work on one part at a time, and then when they get it right (or
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  under other circumstances deterimed by the professor), they can
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  move on to the next part.  Students cannot return to earlier parts
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  once they have been completed.  The score for problem as a whole is
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  made up from the scores on the individual parts, and the relative
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  weighting of the various parts can be specified by the problem
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  author.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  To use the compoundProblem library, use
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#      loadMacros("compoundProblem.pl");
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  at the top of your file, and then create a compoundProblem object
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  via the command
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#      $cp = new compoundProblem(options)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  where '$cp' is the name of a variable that you will use to
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  refer to the compound problem, and 'options' can include:
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    parts => n                The number of parts in the problem.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                Default: 1
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    weights => [n1,...,nm]    The relative weights to give to each
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              part in the problem.  For example,
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                  weights => [2,1,1]
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              would cause the first part to be worth 50%
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              of the points (twice the amount for each of
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              the other two), while the second and third
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              part would be worth 25% each.  If weights
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              are not supplied, the parts are weighted
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              by the number of answer blanks in each part
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              (and you must provide the total number of
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              blanks in all the parts by supplying the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              totalAnswers option).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    totalAnswers => n         The total number of answer blanks in all
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              the parts put together (this is used when
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              computing the per-part scores, if part
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              weights are not provided).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    saveAllAnswers => 0 or 1  Usually, the contents of named answer blanks
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              from previous parts are made available to
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              later parts using variables with the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              same name as the answer blank.  Setting
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              saveAllAnswers to 1 will cause ALL answer
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              blanks to be available (via variables
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              like $AnSwEr1, and so on).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 Default:  0
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    parserValues => 0 or 1    Determines whether the answers from previous
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              parts are returned as MathObjects (like
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              those returned from Real(), Vector(), etc)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              or as strings (the unparsed contents of the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              student answer).  If you intend to use the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              previous answers as numbers, for example,
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              you would want to set this to 1 so that you
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              would get the final result of any formula
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              the student typed, rather than the formula
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              itself as a character string.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 Default:  0
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    nextVisible => type       Tells when the "go on to the next part" option
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              is available to the student.  The possible
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              types include:
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 'ifCorrect'   next is available only when
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               all the answers are correct.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 'Always'      next is always available
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               (but remember that students
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               can't go back once they go
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               on.)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 'Never'       next is never allowed (the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               problem will control going
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               on to the next part itself).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                Default:  'ifCorrect'
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    nextStyle => type         Determines the style of "next" indicator to display
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              (when it is available).  The type can be one of:
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 'CheckBox'    a checkbox that allows the students
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               to go on to the next part when they
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               submit their answers.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 'Button'      a button that submits their answers
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               and goes on to the next part.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 'Forced'      forces the student to go on to the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               next part the next time they submit
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               answers.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                 'HTML'        allows you to provide an arbitrary
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                               HTML string of your own.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                Default:  'Checkbox'
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    nextLabel => string       Specifies the string to use as the label for the checkbox,
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              the name of the button, the text of the message indicating
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              that the next submit will move to the next part, or the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              HTML string, depending on the setting of nextStyle above.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    nextNoChange => 0 or 1    Since the students must submit their answers again to go on
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              to the next part, it is possible for them to change their
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              answers before they submit, and if nextVisible is 'ifCorrect'
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              they might go on to the next without having correct answers
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              stored.  This option lets you control whether the answers
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              are checked against the previous ones before going on to the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              next part.  If the answers don't match, a warning is issued
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              and they are not allowed to move on.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                Default:  1
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    allowReset => 0 or 1      Determines whether a "Go back to the first part" checkbox
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              is provided on parts 2 and later.  This is intended for
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              the professor during testing of the problem (otherwise
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                              it would be impossible to go back to earlier parts).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                Default:  0
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    resetLabel => string      The string used to label the reset checkbox.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Once you have created a compoundProblem object, you can use $cp->part to
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  determine the part that the student is working on, and use 'if' statements
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  to display the proper information for the given part.  The compoundProblem
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  object takes care of maintaining the data as the parts change.  (See the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  compoundProblem.pg file for an example of a compound problem.)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  In order to handle the scoring of the problem as a whole when only part is
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  showing, the compoundProblem object uses its own problem grader to manage
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the scores, and calls your own grader from there.  The default is to use
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the one that was installed before the compoundProblem object was created,
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  or avg_problem_grader if none was installed.  You can specify a different
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  one using the $cp->useGrader() method (see below).  It is important that
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  you NOT call install_problem_grader() yourself once you have created the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  compoundProblem object, as that would disable the special grader, causing
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the compound problem to fail to work properly.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  You may call the following methods once you have a compoundProblem:
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->part                   Returns the part the student is working on.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->part(n)                Sets the part to be part n, as long as the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                student has finished the preceeding parts.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                If not, the part is set to the highest
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                one the student hasn't completed, and he
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                can work up to the given part.  (The
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                nextVisible option is set to 'ifCorrect' if
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                it was 'Never' so that students can go on
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                once they finish the earlier parts.)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->useGrader(code_ref)    Supplies your own grader to use in
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                place of the default one.  For example:
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                  $cp->useGrader(~~&std_problem_grader);
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->score                  Returns the (weighted) score for this part.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                Note that this is the score shown at the bottom
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                of the page on which the student pressed submit
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                (not the score for the answers the student is
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                submitting -- that is not available until
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                after the body of the problem has been created).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->scoreRaw               Returns the unweighted score for this part.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->scoreOverall           Returns the overall score for the problem
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                so far.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->addAnswers(list)       Make additional answer blanks be available
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                from one part to another.  E.g.,
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                   $cp->addAnswers('AnSwEr1');
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                would make the first unnamed blank be available
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                in later parts as well.  (This command should
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                be issued only when the part containing the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                given answer blank is displayed.)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->nextCheckbox(label)    Returns the HTML string for the "go on to next
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                part" checkbox so you can use it in the body of
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                the problem if you wish.  This should not be
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                inserted when the $displayMode is 'TeX'.  If the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                label is not given or is blank, the default label
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                is used.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->nextButton(label)      Returns the HTML string for the "go on to next
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                part" button so you can use it in the body of
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                the problem if you wish.  This should not be
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                inserted when the $displayMode is 'TeX'.  If the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                label is not given or is blank, the default label
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                is used.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->nextForces(label)      Returns the HTML string for the forced "go on to
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                next part" so you can use it in the body of
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                the problem if you wish.  This should not be
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                inserted when the $displayMode is 'TeX'.  If the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                label is not given or is blank, the default label
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                is used.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->reset                  Go back to part 1, clearing the answers
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                and score.  (Best used when debugging problems.)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#    $cp->resetCheckbox(label)   Returns the HTML string for the reset checkbox
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                so that you can provide one within the body
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                of the problem if you wish.  This should not be
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                inserted when the $displayMode is 'TeX'.  If the
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                label is not given or is blank, the default label
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#                                will be used.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  The state data that is stored between invocations of
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the problem.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Create a new instance of the compound Problem and initialize
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  it.  This includes reading the status from the previous
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  parts, defining the variables from the answers to previous parts,
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  and setting up the grader so that the current data can be saved.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Compute the total of the weights so that the parts can
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  be properly scaled.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Look up the status from the previous invocation
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  and see if we need to go on to the next part.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Initialize the current part by setting the ans_rule
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  count (so that later parts will get unique answer names),
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  installing the grader (to save the data), and setting
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the variables for previous answers.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Look through the list of answer labels and set
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the variables for them to be the associated student
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  answer.  Make it a Parser value if requested.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Record the value so that is will be available
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  again on the next invocation.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Look to see is any answers have changed on this
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  invocation of the problem.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Go on to the next part, updating the status
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  to include the data from the old part so that
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  it will be properly preserved when the next
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  part is showing.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Encode all the status information so that it can be
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  maintained as the student submits answers.  Since this
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  state information includes things like the score from
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the previous parts, it is "encrypted" using a dumb
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  hex encoding (making it harder for a student to recognize
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  it as valuable data if they view the page source).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Decode the data and break it into the status hash.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Hex encoding is shifted by 10 to obfuscate it further.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  (shouldn't be a problem since the status will be made of
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  printable characters, so they are all above ASCII 32)
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Make sure the data can be properly preserved within
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  an HTML <INPUT TYPE="HIDDEN"> tag.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Set the grader for this part to the specified one.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Make additional answer blanks from the current part
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  be preserved for use in future parts.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Go back to part 1 and clear the answers and scores.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Return the HTML for the "Go back to part 1" checkbox.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Return the HTML for the "next part" checkbox.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Return the HTML for the "next part" button.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Return the HTML for when going to the next part is forced.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Return the raw HTML provided
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Return the current part, or try to set the part to the given
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  part (returns the part actually set, which may be earlier if
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  the student didn't complete an earlier part).
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  Return the various scores
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  The custom grader that does the work of computing the scores
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#  and saving the data.
./deprecated/compoundProblem.pl:#
./deprecated/PGcomplexmacros.pl:# export functions from Complex1.

./deprecated/PGcomplexmacros.pl:#
./deprecated/PGcomplexmacros.pl:# to your problem in order to use expressions such as 1 +3*i;
./deprecated/PGcomplexmacros.pl:# Without this prototype you would have to write 1+3*i();
./deprecated/PGcomplexmacros.pl:# The prototype has to be defined at compile time.
./deprecated/PGcomplexmacros.pl:# Complex1::display_format('cartesian');
./deprecated/PGcomplexmacros.pl:# number format used frequently in strict prefilters

./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 1) Formats an expression without negative exponents
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#    Thanks to John Jones at ASU for this macro.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 2) Checks for units
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 3) Checks for rounded percents
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 4) Checks both sides of an equation model the word problem.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 5) Checks both sides of an equation for the slope-intercept form of a line.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 6) Checks for function notation in an equality.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 7) Checks factors of polynomials.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# Note: Student's answers must be of the form: monomial(poly)...(poly) with
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#    or without parentheses about the monomial.  The polynomial factors must
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#    not contain any other grouping symbols and, in any case, only parenthesis
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#    may be used.  This needs to be in the instructions for any set that uses
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#    this macro (in the screenHeader.pg file).
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# Note: "StrictFactoringEvaluator" requires leading negatives to be factored out.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#    This not may not work with all situations.  BE SURE TO CHANGE THIS FILE IF THE
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#    FactoringEvaluator IS CHANGED!!
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#---------For diagnosing problems--------------------------------------
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                $ans_hash->setKeys( 'ans_message' =>"FYI: Not checking correctly.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                    AnswerHashScore: $ans_hash->{score} :
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                   Student: $#student_factors, $student_ans,
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                   Formatted: $format_student_ans,
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                   Student Factors: $student_factors[0], $student_factors[1],
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                   : Correct: $#factors, $factors[0], $factors[1],
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                   Number matched: $CorrectFactors");
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#----------------------------------------------------------------------
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 8) Checks for simplified rational expressions.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# Note: Student's answers must be of the form: (poly)/(poly)
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#---------For diagnosing problems--------------------------------------
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#     $ans_hash->setKeys( 'ans_message' =>"FYI: Not checking correctly.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                         Student: $student_num, $student_den,$student_ans;
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#                         Answer: $num,$den,$ans");
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#----------------------------------------------------------------------
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#  9) Writes a fraction in reduced form.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:###################################################################
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:# 10) Checks a list of equations.
./deprecated/CofIdaho_macros.pl:#     Designed for checking a list of asymptotes
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  Routines to make it easier to include additional PG files within
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  a given one.  These files don't have to be in the courseScripts
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  directory; rather, they are assumed to be relative to the directory
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  containing the calling PG file.
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  Usage:  includePGfile(name)
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  where name is the name of a PG file (relative to the directory
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  of the file containing this call).
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  Usage:  includeRandomProblem(file1,file2,...,fileN);
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  where the fileNs are the names of the files from which
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  to choose (relative to the directory of the file containing
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  this call).
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  To use this, make one PG file that include the call to this
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  random routine, and then include it the set definition file
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  as many times as you want (up to N times).  A different problem
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  will be included for each instance in the set definition file.
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#

./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  random seed so that the included problem won't be affected by
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  this function, and replaces it by the psvn, so that the list
./deprecated/unionInclude.pl:#  produced will be the same each time it is called.

./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  Encode all the status information so that it can be
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  maintained as the student submits answers.  Since this
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  state information includes things like the score from
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  the previous parts, it is "encrypted" using a dumb
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  hex encoding (making it harder for a student to recognize
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  it as valuable data if they view the page source).
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  Decode the data and break it into the status hash.
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  Hex encoding is shifted by 10 to obfuscate it further.
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  (shouldn't be a problem since the status will be made of
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  printable characters, so they are all above ASCII 32)
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  Make sure the data can be properly preserved within
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#  an HTML <INPUT TYPE="HIDDEN"> tag.
./deprecated/problemRandomize.pl:#

./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#    Pass it the name of the section and the contents,
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#    or use [$name,options] or {name => ..., options}
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#    as the first argument.
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#  The "section" option defaults to the next section number.
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#  The "iscorrect" option defaults to checking the answers
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#    given in this section.
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#  The "canshow" option defaults to checking if the user
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#    is an instructor or if the answers in all
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#    previous sections are correct.

./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#  plus the names of the answer checkers from the previous sections,

./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#  count from before and after this section (used for highlighting
./deprecated/compoundProblem5.pl:#  the results table), and the rendered text for the section.

./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:# A group of macros used in the Alfred problem library.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:# Given a point (x,y) this macro computes the angle with respect to x-axis. The angle will be between 0 and 2pi.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:## Compute the max and min of an array of numbers
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#This macro prevents students from double clicking in an answer box. This macro #is necessary for multiple integral problems where the answer box is typeset
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#into the integration symbols.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#The problems that have the answer box in the limits must be displayed in JS
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#math mode. This macro warns the user to use JS math mode if they are not.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#This subroutine includes the Strang's textbook into a problem, you have to
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#feed it the chapter and section. It is assumed that the book is in the course
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#directory and is labeled strangtextbook. The parameters are
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#strang(chapter,section,(optional) section title.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#example \{&strang(16,5,"surface integrals")\}.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#Inserts a link to a trig table in the problem.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#example \{&trig_table()\}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#we will associate each student answer with a prime number, noting which student answer is in which blank. This allows us to make use of the fundamental theorem of arithmetic.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#at this point they don't have a matched set of blanks correct. look for a single function in each pair that is right. You have to make sure you only get one for each pair of answer blanks.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### To use the macros your problem must include unionTables.pl, and of course Alfredmacros.pl
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### Table integral returns a string that can be included in a Table to output an integral whose upper and lower limits
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### of integration can be answer blanks. There are several optional parameters:
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### width  - change the width of the answer blanks. defaults to 3.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### lowerwidth  - change the width of the lower answer blank. defaults to width
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### upperwidth  - change the width of the upper answer blank. defaults to width.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### upper - the uppper limit of integration, does not have to be an answer blank, defaults to answer blank with width "width"
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### lower - the lower limit of integration, does not have to be an answer blank,  defaults to answer blank with width "width"
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### limits - boolean, if 1 puts the limits of integration above and below the integral symbol, if 0 puts them after the integral symbol.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###          default is 1.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### Your code must include unionTables.pl, and of course Alfredmacros.pl
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### An example:
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###   \{BeginTable(center=>0).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###      Row([tableintegral(),
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###      ],separation=>2).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###     EndTable();
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###   \}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### which will print an integral with answer blank on the upper and lower limits with the default length of 3
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### This example prints out a double integral, the first integral with answer blanks with width 10, the second integral
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### has 0 for the lower limit of integration and an answer blank with width 5 for the upper limit of integration.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### The default limits of integratin are answer blanks with width 3, in this case the default width was overridden to 5
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### and the default lower limit was changed to a zero.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###   \{BeginTable(center=>0).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###      Row([tableintegral(width=>10,limits=>'\(0\)'),tableintegral(width=>5,lower=>'\(0\)',limits=>0),
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###      ],separation=>2).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###     EndTable();
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###   \}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###  An example where the width of the upper and lower answer blanks have different widths.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###   \{BeginTable(center=>0).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###      Row([tableintegral(lowerwidth=>10,upperwidth=>1)
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###      ],separation=>2).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###     EndTable();
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:###   \}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:# a sum with answer blanks for the summation variable, lower limit, and upper limit
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\{ BeginTable(center=>0).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#     Row([tablesum(width=>10),
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#     ],separation=>2).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#   EndTable();
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:# a sum with answer blanks for the upper and lower limits, and the summation variable is i
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\{ BeginTable(center=>0).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#     Row([tablesum(width=>10,sumvariable=>'i'),
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#     ],separation=>2).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#   EndTable();
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:# a sum with answer blanks for the upper and lower limits, and summation variable is not used.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\{ BeginTable(center=>0).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#     Row([tablesum(width=>10,usesumvariable=>0),
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#     ],separation=>2).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#   EndTable();
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:# sum from n = 1 to infinity
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\{ BeginTable(center=>0).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#      Row([tablesum(sumvariable=>'\(n\)',lower=>'\(1\)', upper=>'\(\hskip 3pt\infty\)') ],separation=>2).
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#   EndTable();
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:#\}
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### Create a vertical bar with an upper and lower limit.
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### Create a subscripted character
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### Create a superscripted character
./deprecated/Alfredmacros.pl:### A fraction
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  This file implements discussion-based questions, where the student
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  can provide essay-style answers, and the professor can make comments
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  on those.  The student and professor can continue to respond to
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  either other, and so carry on a mathematical discussion.  The
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  discussion is private between the professor and student, with
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  each student carrying on a separate discussion with the professor.
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  The professor can view a list of the students in the class with links
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  to their discussions, and indications of how many new messages there
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  are in each.
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  The messages can contain mathematics by enclosing it with \(...\)
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  for in-line mathematics and \[...\] for display-mode math.  The
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  mathematics is entered in TeX format.  It is also possible to use
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  Parser strings that are like the ones students give in normal formula
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  answer blanks.  These are enclosed in `...` or ``...`` for in-line
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  and display modes.  For example `sin(x/(x+1))` would have the same
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  effect as \(\sin\!\left(\frac{x}{x+1}\right)\), but is somewhat easier
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  to read.  [FIXME: a more complete Context() needs to be provided
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  for this.]
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  To make discussions work properly, the professor must set up two files:
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  one called courseStudentList.pg and one called courseProfessorList.pg,
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  with the first containing a list of the userID's of the students in the
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  course and the second containing a list of the professor ID's.  Sample
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  files are provided that you can place in your course templates/macros
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  directory and edit to suit your needs.  Without these files, the
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  professor functions will not operate properly, though the students
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  could still create entries on their own.
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  To start a duscussion, simply assign answerDiscussion.pg to any homework
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  set.  That's it.  The professors can write messages that are visible
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  to all the students, so such a message could be used to provide the
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  starting question for a discussion, for example.  Or the problem could
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  be used by the student to keep a "math journal" for the course (you would
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  want to be sure to keep the homework set open for the whole course in
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  this case).
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  This code is currently considered experimental, and there are still features
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#  the need to be added, but it gives a sense of what is possible.
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:#
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:######################################################################
./deprecated/answerDiscussion.pl:######################################################################

./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#   sqrt_simplify(Num,Natnum,Radnum,Texstr,Error)
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#   Description: Given a natural number, will find the greatest perfect square
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#                that is a factor and use this to simplify the expression:
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#                sqrt(number).
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#   Input :
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#     Num = Number whose sqrt is being taken
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#   Output:
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#     Natnum => Natural number portion of solution (= 1 if no perfect square
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#               divides evenly)
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#     Radnum => The value still inside the radical
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#     Texstr => A LaTex string of the form "Natnum \sqrt{$Radnum}" to be used
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#               for displaying the solution.
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#     Error  => If == 1, then the number entered was bad (ie, negative, nonint)
./deprecated/littleneck.pl:#               (If necessary, this code can be made specific for the error)

./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#Name: perlround
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#Input: a number to round, then a place to round to. e.g. 2=>hundredths, 0=>whole, -1=>tens
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#Output: $x rounded to the $n place. This attempts to overcome quirks with rounding when the cut part is like 0.005
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:################################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#Name: RandomName
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#Input: None required
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#Optional Input: 'sex' => male or female
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#Output: A name that is randomly chosen from the list maintained here.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   If 'sex' is specified, the name is from the list of that sex.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:################################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Name: RandomVariableName
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Input: None required
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: 'type' => variable, constant, integer, or function
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Output: A variable name like x, y, t, c, etc. that is randomly chosen from the list maintained here.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         If 'type' is specified, the name is from the list of that type.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Sample use:
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:################################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Name: xPower
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# This is just an auxiliary subroutine for the polynomial macros that follow
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:################################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Name: PolyString
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Input: an arrays
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  PolyString(~~@poly1)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Arrays elements should be numerical or Math Objects. The idea is that
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# these are coefficients of a polynomial, either starting from the
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# constant term or from the leading term.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: order=>ascending or descending, defaults to descending
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   order is ignored if output=>array
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: var=> some string, defaulting to "x"
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Output: an polynomial string using var and ready to be fed to Compute
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         For example, given
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             @poly1 = (1,2);  # represents x+2
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             @poly2 = (3,-1,0,4);
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         PolyString(~~@poly1);
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             'x^1+2x^0'
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         PolyString(~~@poly2,order=>ascending,var=>'z');
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             '3x^0+-1x^1+0x^2+4x^3'
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         Note that instance of +-, x^0, x^1, 0x^n, may be present,
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         but Math Objectification will handle that.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Note: If you intend to feed the output string to Compute() or Formula(),
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  you should set the context reductions appropriately and apply ->reduce
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  *twice* to remove excess parentheses from negative coefficients.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:################################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Name: PolyMult
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Input: two arrays, for example
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  PolyMult(~~@poly1, ~~@poly2)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Arrays elements should be numerical or Math Objects
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# where multiplication and addition are allowed. The idea is that
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# these are coefficients of a polynomial, either starting from the
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# constant term or from the leading term.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: order=>ascending or descending, defaults to descending
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   order is ignored if output=>array
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: var=> some string, defaulting to "x"
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: output=>array, simplified, unsimplified
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   which is either an array of coefficients, an expanded and simplified
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   polynomial string using var and ready to be fed to Compute, or the same
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   but unsimplified. Default is array.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# cmh, 6/18/13
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: exponentVar=> some string, defaulting to ''
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   to be used if the polynomial looks like, for example
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#            x^{3n}+x^{2n}+x^{n}
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Output: described in Optional Input
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         For example, given
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             @poly1 = (1,2);  # represents x+2
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             @poly2 = (3,-1,4);  # represents 3x^2-x+4
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         PolyMult(~~@poly1, ~~@poly2,
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             order=>ascending, var=>'z', output=>unsimplified)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             '3z^3+-1z^2+4z+6z^2+-2z^1+8z^0'
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         Note that instance of +- may be present, but Math Objectification
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         will handle that.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         Given
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#           @poly1 = (Fraction(1,3),2);  # represents 1/3x+2
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#           @poly2 = (Fraction(1,2),Fraction(-1,3));  # represents 1/2x-1/3
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         PolyMult(~~@poly1, ~~@poly2)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             (Frac(1,6),Frac(8,9),Frac(-1,9))
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Note: If you intend to feed the output string to Compute() or Formula(),
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  you should set the context reductions appropriately and apply ->reduce
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  *twice* to remove excess parentheses from negative coefficients.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:################################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Accepts an array of the form that PolyTerms outputs
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Inserts '&' between array entries for use in array environments
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Converts Math Object entries to their TeX form
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Inserts a spacing command after nonempty entries - the command
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   \setlength\arraycolsep{0em} should precede the \begin{array}
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# $Spacing should be something like "\hspace{0.4em}"
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Also creates an alignment string for the \begin{array} command
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# $align should be "r", "c", or "l"
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Not part of PGpolynomialMacros.pl
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# PolyTerms(~~@coefficientArray,x,order)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Accepts an array containing the coefficients of a polynomial
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   in descending order
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Assumes coefficients have numerical values, but may be Math Objects
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Returns an array of size 2n-1 of the form
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   (a_nx^n,  +/-,  |a_{n-1}| x^{n-1},  +/-,  ...)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   where the variable is x is the second argument, the terms are Math Objects
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   Formulas, and the +/- are Perl strings.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# The third argument can be "ascending" to reverse the order
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# If a_j is 0, then the corresponding term and preceding sign are empty
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   strings.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Trims beginning and ending, so that first and last terms are nonzero
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Not part of PGpolynomialMacros.pl
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#=cut
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Name: PolySub
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Name: PolyAdd
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Input: two arrays, for example
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  PolyAdd(~~@poly1, ~~@poly2)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Arrays elements should be numerical or Math Objects
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# where addition are allowed. The idea is that
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# these are coefficients of a polynomial, either starting from the
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# constant term or from the leading term.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: order=>ascending or descending, defaults to descending
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   order is ignored if output=>array
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: var=> some string, defaulting to "x"
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: subtract=> 1 will subtract; default 0
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: output=>array, simplified, unsimplified
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   which is either an array of coefficients, a simplified
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   polynomial string using var and ready to be fed to Compute, or the same
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#   but unsimplified with like terms near each other. Default is array.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Output: described in Optional Input
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         For example, given
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             @poly1 = (1,2);  # represents x+2 (assuming descending)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             @poly2 = (3,-1,4);  # represents 3x^2-x+4
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         PolyAdd(~~@poly1, ~~@poly2,var=>'z', output=>unsimplified)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             '3z^2+1z+-1z+2+4'
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         Note that instance of +- may be present, but Math Objectification
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         will handle that.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         Given
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#           @poly1 = (Fraction(1,3),2);  # represents 1/3+2x (assuming ascending)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#           @poly2 = (Fraction(1,2),0,Fraction(-1,3));
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#         PolyAdd(~~@poly1, ~~@poly2,order=>ascending)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#             (Frac(5,6),2,Frac(-1,3))
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Note: If you intend to feed the output string to Compute() or Formula(),
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  you should set the context reductions appropriately and apply ->reduce
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  *twice* to remove excess parentheses from negative coefficients.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:################################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:###############################
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Name: numberWord
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Input: either a whole number <100 or a fraction with numerator and denominator less than 100. If fraction, set denominator parameter.
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: capital => 1 (default is 0)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Optional Input: denominator=> 4 (default is 1)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#  numberWord($n)
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:# Output: a string spelling that number
./misc/PCCmacros.pl:#
